# Sports research local operating procedure — v24.15

Status: **LOCAL CHAT WORKFLOW / READ-ONLY GITHUB / NOT AN ISSUING WRITER**  
Reviewed on **11 November 2026**. Governing repository `danisgreat/Sports-Research`, pinned main commit `4d8bd1771d20974bcbfc7c694a009cd44e95a693`. Reference baseline for change review: `f79559b45183ca63a2ce5fc2611321975bae744f`.

This is a **local procedure for this assistant's interactions and downloadable artifacts**. It does not alter the official repository, its rules, model releases, mini-log writer lease or original research. For each new task, refresh GitHub main, compare the current release, and obey any newer controlling protocol. A pinned copy is a snapshot, not a durable permission to use stale controls.

## 1 — Authority, version checks and modes

1. First read `CURRENT_STATE.md`, `CORE.md`, `research/prompts/1_GAME_CARD.md`, the applicable `research/prompts/1_sports/<SPORT>.md`, and relevant sport rules from one **current pinned full commit SHA**. The current index is [EXT-v3](https://github.com/danisgreat/Sports-Research/blob/4d8bd1771d20974bcbfc7c694a009cd44e95a693/EXTERNAL_RESEARCH_PROTOCOL_V3.md) with [profile](https://github.com/danisgreat/Sports-Research/blob/4d8bd1771d20974bcbfc7c694a009cd44e95a693/research/external/EXTERNAL_PROFILE_v24.15.md), [lane briefs](https://github.com/danisgreat/Sports-Research/blob/4d8bd1771d20974bcbfc7c694a009cd44e95a693/research/external/LANE_BRIEFS_v24.15.md) and [upload index](https://github.com/danisgreat/Sports-Research/blob/4d8bd1771d20974bcbfc7c694a009cd44e95a693/research/external/README.md).
2. Treat the release as `MDS-2026.11.11-v24.15`, `CR-2026.11.11-R3`, prompt suite `P-2026.11.11-v24.13`, `EXT-v3`, `REQ-v5`, kit `SR-KIT-v24.15`; scoring remains `SCV-2026.10.22-v12` and selection `SEL-2026.10.22-v4`, until a new main commit changes them.
3. GitHub interaction is **read/query/fetch only**, per the user's standing instruction. Never create, edit, delete, comment, commit, push, run GitHub Actions, change PRs/issues/branches, or imply that a GitHub append was completed. The official issuing writer is the designated repository agent holding the mini lease.
4. If an owner-provided, pinned `EVENT/CONTRACTS/REQUIREMENTS/ARTIFACTS` bundle, EV2 key, manifest SHA, exact input-package SHA and assigned lane are available, work **only the assigned role** and format an exact LANE_RETURN. If absent, conduct independent **READ_ONLY_DRAFT** research, not a fabricated EXT-v3 lane receipt, `FULL_FIVE` claim or issued card. Do not self-assign EV2 keys, event slots or canonical P-IDs.
5. Formal EXT-v3 runs use manifest-hashed `FULL_FIVE`, declared `REDUCED` (2–4 assigned roles including L5) or `FULL_SIX` (L6 audit) as the repository coordinator specifies. Never simulate parallel chats; L4 waits for accepted L1, L5 waits for all assigned upstream roles and collector review. Chat agreement does not establish independent sources. `REDUCED` and `FULL_SIX` are separate evaluation cohorts.

## 2 — Exact event, source and clock checks

1. Normalize each user-provided event: sport/competition/season, official home/away participants, native ID if found, venue, local/UTC/AEDT start, status, and **each supplied contract's exact label, period, unit, endpoint, operator action**. REQ-v5 adds home/venue, explicit ML-vs-spread, tennis surface/best-of, cricket innings/toss and `Rank: supplied lines only | all lines`. Older REQ-v4 blocks remain valid: research missing details and disclose them. Default rank scope `SUPPLIED_ONLY`.
2. Before each online page opening, check the applicable **ChatGPT-User** access status and exact path; `PARTLY_BLOCKED` is path-specific. Use current access registry `research/external/access/CURRENT_ACCESS.csv`; the copies checked at `2026-11-10T23:31:54+00:00` expire at `2026-11-17T23:31:54+00:00`, and require an actual new audit after expiry. Blocked or unreadable rules mean **do not open**; no cache, mirror, proxy, translation, user-agent substitution or login workaround. Allowed but inaccessible pages are `TECHNICAL_FAILURE`, followed by permitted alternatives. Follow site terms as well.
3. Do not search or use bookmaker odds, betting/tipster previews, line movements or market-derived adjustments; supplied lines are contract definitions, not evidence. X/Instagram/Facebook may only enter as documented owner captures (one original source) where permitted. Use official match/league sites, legitimate reporters, dated results, and documented collector lineage.
4. Record **actual** research-start, per-source retrieval and completion timestamps with UTC offsets using `datetime.now(timezone.utc)` when code execution is available. If not, `CLOCK_UNAVAILABLE`. An undated page has `published_at: UNKNOWN`; a *verified* pre-start retrieval only establishes availability by that retrieval, not earlier publication. A post-start retrieval cannot be presented as pre-start evidence. A live score/outcome exposure prevents a clean pregame admission.
5. Check actual start/status separately. Official delays use the coordinator's append-only, independently sourced delay amendment (new start in the future, explicit no-play evidence); do not rewrite the original manifest or make a started event pregame. Under uncertainty, preserve research, mark its timing faithfully and abstain from a clean pre-start forecast claim.

## 3 — Evidence and probability release

1. First verify both teams' confirmed availability: expected/confirmed starters, playing bench, absences, injury mechanisms, suspensions and late roster changes. **Projected is not confirmed.** For hockey, actual starting goalies; for cricket, toss and innings; for tennis, court and best-of; and indoor/outdoor match conditions as relevant.
2. Reconstruct exact-competition team/player history as of the evidence cutoff: season, L5/L10/L15/L20 with actual sample size `n`, opponents, scores, dates, home/away, exclusions, scheduling/rest and same-season matchup. Overlapping windows are not independent trials; if only `n=2`, display `n=2`, not a manufactured L20. Preserve source URL/class, publication/retrieval times, direct field values and original collector identity.
3. Treat uncorroborated, conflicting, late or unreachable facts as named gaps. Run appropriate league reference/competition-specific baseline **only if executable and properly evidenced**. v24.15 contains 32 measured soccer league parameter rows, but they are descriptive uncalibrated rows; `TEAM-CENTRE-v1` is **SHADOW ONLY**. No new injury, goalie, weather or pitcher effect is automatically estimated.
4. For an authorized L5 price, require the matching pinned `SR-KIT-v24.15`, parameters, entire receipt, coherent single-event distribution, sourced adjustments and repo writer's independent receipt verification. Cricket simulation and tennis form shock remain `SENSITIVITY_ONLY`; a hockey structural check does not prove calibration. Never invent 100,000 simulations or numeric `p_card` values.
5. Every supplied contract gets **exactly one disposition**: verified numeric probability (where admitted) **or** reasoned `UNPRICED` (`UNSUPPORTED`, `UNAVAILABLE`, `ALL_PUSH`, `NOT_VALIDATED`). Do not invent 50% placeholders, omit contracts, mark missing required facts DONE, or rank unpriced rows. Qualitative standalone directions are clearly `UNCALIBRATED_QUALITATIVE` and do not qualify for scoring.
6. Basketball totals and spreads include overtime by the existing BK-OT-1 convention; model overtime as actual extra scoring by both sides. NHL/AHL regulation versus OT/SO and shootout-goal operator terms require explicit declaration; no silent assumption of a scoring endpoint. Preserve push mass and other contract actions.

## 4 — External-role custody and local mini

1. Central writer alone prepares, hashes and commits event artifacts (`EVENT.csv`, `CONTRACTS.csv`, `REQUIREMENTS.csv`, `ARTIFACTS.csv`, `POLICY.csv`), generates exact packets, reviews sources, admits raw LANE_RETURN blocks, freezes any eligible SINGLE before exposure, freezes FIVE after accepted lane evidence, appends one card or research-only entry and validates learning/ledger/bind. A standalone ChatGPT draft **does not** perform any of those official transitions.
2. For a genuine assigned role, copy exact `event_key`, `lane`, `chat_label`, `profile_version`, commit SHA, manifest and packet hashes, actual clocks and outcome-exposure status. Emit all required `[EVIDENCE]`, `[ACCESS_LOG]`, `[REQUIREMENTS]`, and L5 `[PROBABILITIES]` / `[UNPRICED]` with one exact `<!-- BEGIN LANE_RETURN -->` block. Use field/requirement IDs supplied by the coordinator; do not invent them.
3. Preserve locally staged independent research in the local working copy; **do not allocate the repository's local event keys**. Before appending, search all existing intake and draft bodies for event identity duplicates, and preserve the old six drafts and their source context. No retrospective reclassification of prior research as eligible v24.15 forecasts. A correction is dated and parented, not a silent edit of a frozen forecast.
4. `20261109R3NEXT` remains `ACTIVE / EMPTY` on published GitHub at this review. The previous six notes in the local copy are `DRAFT-001` through `DRAFT-006`, **not issued**, not inserted into the authoritative mini, and candidate `G0001`–`G0006` are unreserved. The next *potential* local draft label is `DRAFT-007`; this is a local ordering hint, not an official identity or an admission claim.
5. Use `READ_ONLY_DRAFT / NOT_ISSUED`, accurate event timing, the qualitative vs numeric classification, sources and an A1–A9 coverage matrix. Keep the active GitHub footer's canonical next P-ID untouched. Downloadable artifacts are created locally and linked only after verifying their actual paths.

## 5 — Controls and limits of this review

- The latest two commits add EXT-v2 then EXT-v3 and related toolkit/statistical/source revisions. This local assistant is **not connected to the owner's Windows clone** (`C:\Users\danie\Projects\Sports-Research`) and cannot claim to have executed its tools, full test suite, real five-chat pilot, native file writes, commits or pushes.
- The experiment's confirmatory question is FIVE_LANES vs SINGLE_RESEARCH on the same frozen contracts and evidence horizon, with at least 100 eligible paired settled events over at least 8 settled ISO weeks, and paired Brier improvement of at least 0.01 plus interval/calibration checks. Neither a chat pilot nor predictive superiority has been demonstrated.
- This memo is an **operating-note update**, not an official repo change, model recalibration, scored prediction, or user account Memory modification. Re-read the current main on the next sports request.

# GitHub change review and local adaptation — 11 November 2026

Repository: https://github.com/danisgreat/Sports-Research  
Old reviewed HEAD: `f79559b45183ca63a2ce5fc2611321975bae744f`  
New reviewed HEAD: `4d8bd1771d20974bcbfc7c694a009cd44e95a693`  
Comparison: https://github.com/danisgreat/Sports-Research/compare/f79559b45183ca63a2ce5fc2611321975bae744f...4d8bd1771d20974bcbfc7c694a009cd44e95a693

**Verified:** two newer commits, first `28978e1` (v24.14 / EXT-v2) and second `4d8bd17` (v24.15 / EXT-v3). Latest governing method/control/prompt: `MDS-2026.11.11-v24.15 / CR-2026.11.11-R3 / P-2026.11.11-v24.13`. Scoring/selection versions remain `SCV-2026.10.22-v12 / SEL-2026.10.22-v4`.

## Findings and corresponding local process updates

| Finding from repository | Local change applied |
|---|---|
| EXT-v3 supersedes EXT-v2 and offers FULL_FIVE, explicit REDUCED (2–4 roles, including L5), FULL_SIX; one writer and immutable event package | Documented scope gates and the distinction between one standalone research chat and a real, admitted multi-lane run |
| Source rules are reader-specific (ChatGPT-User versus Claude-User; scripts all eight); source snapshot expiry separately maintained | Source access gate before web page opening, path-specific check, no forbidden workaround; respect original observation/expiry |
| REQ-v5 has home/venue, exact line type, tennis surface/best-of, cricket format/toss, ranking scope and optional lane/mini/commit | Formal event/contract normalization; older REQ-v4 accepted with actual researched resolution of missing fields |
| Unpublished source date UNKNOWN may be admitted only with actual pre-start retrieval | Evidence clock/provenance policy; no backdated retrieval or presumed publication |
| Official start delays need original-time-retaining, sourced, hash-chained amendments | Preserve scheduled/effective start and no-play proof; cannot upgrade post-start research |
| Single-research comparison freezes before packet exposure; no real five-chat pilot completed | No false prospective claim from six prior local drafts; comparison remains unpaired absent coordinator receipt |
| L5 may assign each supplied contract verified numeric or exact UNPRICED; zero-numeric research-only allowed | Record every contract and why numerical pricing is unsupported; abstain/qualitative without fabricated p |
| 32 measured soccer competition rows installed; cricket aggregates rebuilt and corrected; TEAM-CENTRE shadow candidate | Do not use old generic soccer constants where a measured row applies; do not promote shadow, fitted, or unvalidated simulations |
| More rigorous exact-source/collector lineage and later factual settlement scorecards | Record collector identity and partial factual coverage; no majority vote, retrospective learning or late fact as pre-start input |
| First v24.15 chat profile/kit are a matching versioned bundle | Keep one complete committed version; refuse mismatched hashes and incomplete artifacts |

**Local artifact check:** six staged drafts existed in `research/active_minis/20261109R3NEXT_LOCAL_DRAFT.md`, with six notes, six intake entries, no actual ID assignment or issuance and official footer preserved. Verified original file SHA-256 `d3754133f1399f52cf5d2b9113cb6e44f9cc9ab395d5c97dbd10328f3e2a08b6`. A distinct v24.15-marked copy retains that entire file as an exact byte prefix; a new separate procedure memo provides current rules. The repo mini itself remains `ACTIVE / EMPTY` at the reviewed current HEAD. No merge, commit or push was performed.
